## Is the difference more than noise?

288 pairs of games: A's margin is +59 VP a game against B's, give or take 158 VP (two standard errors). Verdict: within noise (could be chance).

### Deployment: positions vs output by unit type: ON as Opponent

Output = kills + objectives per 100 of its own points (units in brackets). Kills = the Victory Points of the enemy units it put out of the battle (a fight's points shared by the wounds caused); objectives = the markers, hill, baggage and trophies it scored (game logs from 2026-11-08 on; quarters at the end of the battle belong to no one unit). Acted = fought, shot or cast; round = the mean round of its first action. Joined characters count where their unit deployed.
